Lanes A to E start the same day. Lane B and C releases wait on RGL-07 and RGL-08 to publish. Lane F starts after 2.4.0 ships. RGL-52 precedes RGL-24's binding/drop break and RGL-35's identities/class timing, as well as RGL-26, RGL-32, RGL-50 and RGL-38. The two Phase 5 prerequisites, RGL-52 and the unwired RGL-23, can proceed in parallel; neither can move past the RGL-24 join alone. RGL-51 and RGL-53 gate release. RGL-46 spans the two release-specific fixture lanes without editing consumer-2.3.

### File overlaps

RGL-52 prepares legacy absorption before RGL-24 wires the binding, RGL-26 changes responsive width arguments, RGL-32 consolidates drop props, RGL-35 changes class timing/identities, RGL-50 removes modern surface and RGL-38 flips the wrapper default. The overlap table uses that dependency order even where the PR IDs are out of order. Breaking PRs that adapt private legacy paths list those files too; the rows below include those atomic updates. The migration tooling and guide are separate new paths (RGL-51/RGL-53).

These files appear in more than one PR. PRs in the same row must merge in the listed order, or rebase on each other.

| `test/e2e/scenarios.spec.ts`                | 06, 27, 31, 39                                                 | RGL-27 and RGL-31 list `test/e2e/*`.                                                                           |

| `test/e2e/__frames__/*`                     | 06, 24, 27, 31                                                 |                                                                                                                |

---

## 10. Failure Matrix

Columns: the state or input, what the operation does, how it can fail, and what the caller is told. "Caller" means the consumer's callbacks and console, not the engine's internal state. "Stop callback (cancelled)" is shorthand for `onDragStop`/`onResizeStop` with `(startLayout, startItem, startItem, null, e, el, detail)`, `detail.cancelled: true` and a `cancelReason` (§4, rank 4). `startLayout` there never contains an item removed during the interaction.

Rows marked **(today: pinned)** have a #2321 trace of today's behavior, which RGL-23 and RGL-24 list as an expected diff (§9 Phase 5). Rows marked **(2.4.0)** apply from 2.4.0. All other rows apply from 3.0.0.

### Interaction identity

| State or input                                                                  | What the operation does                                                                                                                        | How it can fail                                                                                | What the caller is told                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **`propose`, `stop` or `cancel` with an id that is not `active.interactionId`** | No-op: the same state, plus a `warning` event `RGL_W011`.                                                                                      | A stale or foreign gesture moves or ends the active interaction.                               | `RGL_W011` once per grid, in development. Nothing else.                         |
| **Move or stop with `active === null`**                                         | The same rule: no-op plus `RGL_W011`.                                                                                                          | A stale gesture moves a committed item.                                                        | `RGL_W011` in development.                                                      |
| **Keyboard nudge while a pointer drag or resize is active**                     | The `nudge` action is a no-op while `active` is set. It carries no id the active interaction could match.                                      | The nudge moves the dragged item to the nudge cell, and its stop commits the drag mid-gesture. | Nothing; `announce` says the item is busy when set. The pointer drag continues. |
| **Second pointer on another item while dragging**                               | Its `start` is rejected, so the binding never gets an id for it and puts that gesture in ignore-until-release.                                 | Item A follows item B's pointer.                                                               | Nothing. No second `onDragStart`.                                               |
| **HTML5 drop hover while a pointer drag is active**                             | The drop `start` is rejected. No `dragover` dispatches a `propose`, because the controller holds no id.                                        | Each `dragover` moves the dragged item.                                                        | Nothing. No placeholder.                                                        |
| **Mouse and touch drop sources at once**                                        | The first `start{kind:"drop"}` wins. The second source gets no id, so its hover moves and its `cancel("dropLeft")` never reach the first drop. | Two placeholders, or the second source cancels the first drop.                                 | Nothing.                                                                        |
| **Hook moveDrag with a stale or foreign interactionId**                         | The explicit interactionId does not match active.interactionId, so propose hits the identity rule.                                             | A foreign hook action moves the active item.                                                   | `RGL_W011` in development.                                                      |

### Engine lifecycle and cancellation

| State or input                                                                                                      | What the operation does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | How it can fail                                                                                                       | What the caller is told                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Grid unmounts mid-drag or mid-resize**                                                                            | The ref, the store and its listeners are dropped together. RD and RR remove their document listeners on unmount (`DraggableCore.tsx:289-293`). `GridItem` effect cleanup stops the edge-scroll loop and clears timers (RX-10a fix).                                                                                                                                | The rAF loop keeps running (RX-10a today). A `setState` lands after unmount.                                          | Nothing. Unmounting the grid is the one case that skips the stop callback. Documented.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **StrictMode effect replay at mount**                                                                               | React runs effect cleanup and setup again with no render between. The store lives in a ref for the component's lifetime, so nothing is disposed or recreated. `useSyncExternalStore` unsubscribes and resubscribes to the same store. The mount effect finds its "events sent" flag set and emits nothing.                                                         | A subscription to a disposed store; two mount notifications.                                                          | Same callbacks as non-strict mode. #2321 has StrictMode variants; from RGL-24 every scenario also runs in StrictMode, and a drag right after a StrictMode mount with no rerender is its own test.                                                                                         |
| **Effects re-run without unmount mid-gesture** (Suspense re-suspend, React 19 `<Activity mode="hidden">`)           | The store persists. `GridItem` cleanup stops the edge-scroll loop and timers. When the grid's dedicated empty-deps layout effect re-runs and finds `active` set, it dispatches `cancel("pointercancel")`, because a hidden subtree cannot hold a gesture. The config and props effect never dispatches this cancel; it cancels only when `interactionKey` changed. | The interaction stays active with no gesture behind it, or every controlled update cancels the drag.                  | Stop callback (cancelled) with `cancelReason: "pointercancel"`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| **Stop before start** (#2291, CHANGELOG.md:18; today: pinned by the 2.3.0 fix)                                      | A release while activation is still pending dispatches nothing. A release after `start` but before the start render commits dispatches `stop` with the id `dispatch` returned, so the store handles it.                                                                                                                                                            | A drag that never ends; a placeholder that stays.                                                                     | Pending activation: nothing, and no `onDragStart`. After `start`: `onDragStop` once, as the 2.3.0 fix does. RGL-39 adds the delay-rule variant.                                                                                                                                           |
| **Stop with no proposal** (a click after activation)                                                                | Commits `startLayout` unchanged. The pipeline does not run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | A click moves an item.                                                                                                | `onDragStop` with the start item. No `onLayoutChange`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **Same-cell move**                                                                                                  | The same state, one `move` event.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `onDrag` stops firing per pointer move, a call-count change.                                                          | `onDrag` once per pointer move, as today (`GridItem.tsx:565`). 0 `GridLayout` renders (T7).                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| **Active item's child removed mid-drag** (today: pinned, `dragged-child-removed-mid-drag.v2`)                       | The removed `GridItem`'s cleanup only removes its own listeners and timers; it never dispatches. The grid's layout effect then dispatches `syncProps` without the id. The reducer cancels with `itemRemoved`, with `startLayout` minus the item as the target, then applies the sync. One path, so the order is deterministic.                                     | The engine keeps moving a ghost. The placeholder stays. Two cancels. The stop callback's layout brings the item back. | Stop callback (cancelled) with `cancelReason: "itemRemoved"`, argument 1 without the item, argument 6 the detached node. Then one `onLayoutChange` for the layout without the item.                                                                                                       |
| **Another child removed, then the drag stops**                                                                      | The removal applies at once to `workingLayout` and `startLayout`. The step re-runs on `lastProposal`, so the dragged item can move into the freed space. Stop commits the stop pipeline's result.                                                                                                                                                                  | The removed item stays as an invisible obstacle.                                                                      | Nothing during the drag. `onDragStop` and one `onLayoutChange`, neither containing the item.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **Another child removed, then the interaction is cancelled** (Escape, touch cancel, stop veto, error)               | The cancel target is `startLayout`, which already lost the item.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | The revert brings the deleted item back as an obstacle with no child.                                                 | Stop callback (cancelled), argument 1 without the item. One `onLayoutChange` without the item, because the committed layout changed.                                                                                                                                                      |
| **Child added mid-drag**                                                                                            | The modern binding uses the explicit layout or its default for the child id. Legacy constructs data-grid items before sync. The sync goes to pending (queue rule 3).                                                                                                                                                                                               | The new item overlaps the dragged item.                                                                               | Applied at the flush, compacted, one `onLayoutChange`. `RGL_W003`, because it changes geometry.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| **`layout` prop changes mid-drag** (today: pinned, `controlled-layout-mid-drag`)                                    | The newest controlled layout waits in `pending`; its `data` is patched at once. At the flush it applies after the interaction result.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | The drag result silently overwrites the new prop, or an older pending layout wins over a newer one.                   | `onDragStop` reports the drag result. The flushed sync commits, `RGL_W003` once if its geometry differs, and one `onLayoutChange` reports the final layout.                                                                                                                               |
| **Controlled parent renders `onDrag`'s layout every move**                                                          | Each update replaces `pending.layout`. At release it equals `lastEmitted`, so flush step 3 drops it and the stop pipeline's result stands, including a stop edit or veto.                                                                                                                                                                                          | A warning on every frame, or a stale layout applied after stop.                                                       | A normal stop: `onDragStop` and one `onLayoutChange` with the drag result. A vetoed stop: the cancelled `onDragStop` at `startLayout` and no `onLayoutChange`. An edited stop: `onDragStop` and `onLayoutChange` with the edit. No `RGL_W003` in any of the three. RGL-24 pins all three. |
| **Controlled parent one step stale at release** (two moves before one parent render; the measured master case)      | The parent's layout is the newest controlled layout, so it wins at release.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `onDragStop` and the DOM disagree, as on master today (`onDragStop` x=2, DOM x=1).                                    | `onDragStop` reports the gesture; `RGL_W003` once; one `onLayoutChange` carries the parent's final layout. RGL-24's batching test pins it on React 18 and 19.                                                                                                                             |
| **External layout B, then a newer controlled layout C, then stop**                                                  | C replaces B in `pending`. At release C applies.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Stale B overwrites C on release.                                                                                      | `onDragStop` with the drag result, then one `onLayoutChange` with C applied; `RGL_W003` if C's geometry differs from the drag result.                                                                                                                                                     |
| **Parent restores an earlier layout mid-drag** (for example after a server rejects a cell)                          | It is the newest controlled layout, so it wins at release.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | The intended override is dropped.                                                                                     | `RGL_W003` and one `onLayoutChange` at the flush. RGL-23 pins it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| **Debounced `onDrag` update lands after stop**                                                                      | An idle sync; it applies and wins, as a late prop update does today.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | The item moves back to the debounced cell.                                                                            | One `onLayoutChange` for the synced layout. The docs say not to feed throttled `onDrag` output back; RGL-23 pins it.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **Data-only prop change, idle**                                                                                     | `sameInput` differs, so the binding dispatches `syncProps`; the new `data` references reach state. `sameGeometry` matches, so there is no notification.                                                                                                                                                                                                            | The change is dropped, and `renderItem` and middleware keep stale `data`; or it fires `onLayoutChange` and loops.     | Nothing. `renderItem` sees the new `data` on the next render.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **Data-only prop change, mid-drag**                                                                                 | Queue rule 3 patches the new `data` references, by id, onto every engine layout at once. The layout itself is pending and applies at release: a parent that rendered the drag's steps keeps the drag result, and one that held its pre-drag geometry gets that geometry back, with `RGL_W003`.                                                                     | The data is lost at stop, or `renderItem` shows stale `data` mid-drag.                                                | `renderItem` sees the new `data` mid-drag. `onDragStop` reports the gesture; one `onLayoutChange` only if the final layout differs from the last notified one.                                                                                                                            |
| **`cols`, compactor or constraints change mid-drag**                                                                | `setConfig` goes to `pending.config`. `state.config` stays frozen while `active` is set. Flushed on stop and cancel, before the layout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | The drag reads the new `cols` half way through.                                                                       | Applied after the interaction ends. `RGL_W003` only when `cols`, `maxRows` or the compactor key changed (§3 engine config); a constraints change applies silently.                                                                                                                        |
| **Inline `gridConfig` or compactor spread every render**                                                            | `sameConfig` compares values, so the binding dispatches nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | A config change, and a resync, on every parent render; `RGL_W003` on every controlled drag.                           | Nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **Middleware or constraint object rebuilt every render** (`middleware={[snap(5)]}`)                                 | A new element reference is a config change by definition. Idle: stored, no resync. Mid-drag: held in `pending.config`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Not a failure the engine can tell from a real change.                                                                 | Nothing; it applies at the flush (§3 engine config). The docs say to create the object once.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| **Breakpoint change mid-drag** (today: pinned, `responsive-breakpoint-mid-drag.strict`)                             | `ResponsiveGridLayout` passes the breakpoint as `interactionKey`. When it changes during an interaction, the binding dispatches `cancel("breakpointChange")` before `setConfig` and `syncProps` from the same effect.                                                                                                                                              | The active item keeps coordinates in the old column count, or the change queues behind the drag.                      | `onBreakpointChange`, `onLayoutChange` and `onWidthChange` from the responsive effect (`ResponsiveGridLayout.tsx:329,399,416`), then the stop callback (cancelled) with `cancelReason: "breakpointChange"`. RGL-24 pins the order.                                                        |
| **Breakpoint change mid-drag through `useResponsiveLayout` + `GridLayout`**                                         | With `interactionKey={breakpoint}`, the same cancel. Without it, the change queues like any prop change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | The two responsive paths behave differently.                                                                          | As above with the key. Documented in the hook guide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **Touch cancel** (`touchcancel`; today: pinned, `internal-touch-cancel.touch`)                                      | The binding dispatches `cancel("pointercancel")`. The engine reverts to `startLayout`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | The drag is stuck, the analog of D-R3. The placeholder remains.                                                       | Stop callback (cancelled) with `cancelReason: "pointercancel"`. No `onLayoutChange` (net-zero).                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| **Engine cancels or rejects while RD or RR still holds the gesture**                                                | The binding marks the item "ignore until release". Its handlers return early, the `react-draggable-dragging` class comes off, and the local pixel box resets to the committed cell.                                                                                                                                                                                | Later moves and the stop reach the reducer with a stale id, or restart a drag.                                        | Nothing beyond the row that caused it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| **Ignore-until-release never sees a stop** (mouse released outside the window, D-R3 or D-R10 on the RD 4.5.0 floor) | The state also clears on `pointerup`, `pointercancel` or `blur` on `window`, and after 30 s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | The item ignores every later gesture until a reload.                                                                  | Nothing. A Playwright scenario cancels, releases outside the window and drags again.                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| **Mouse released outside the window** (today: to pin in Playwright)                                                 | RD listens on `ownerDocument` in the capture phase (`DraggableCore.tsx:378-379`). Browsers normally deliver the `mouseup`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | No `mouseup` arrives, so the drag continues on re-entry with no button held.                                          | Pinned first. If Playwright shows a stuck drag, the binding treats a `mousemove` with `buttons === 0` as a stop.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| **Escape key mid-drag**                                                                                             | Only with the `keyboard` prop (`cancelOnEscape`): a document listener dispatches `cancel("escape")`, and the engine reverts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Without `keyboard`, Escape does nothing, as today.                                                                    | Stop callback (cancelled) with `cancelReason: "escape"`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| **Start on a static item**                                                                                          | Rejected by the permission function, unless the item sets a boolean `isDraggable` (drag) or `isResizable` (resize), which wins over `static` and the grid flag, as today (`GridLayout.tsx:1180-1186`, `core/layout.ts:277`). Pointer and keyboard starts use the same function.                                                                                    | A supported override is rejected, or a pointer start and a nudge decide differently.                                  | Nothing for a plain static. Normal callbacks for an override.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| **Start on `isDraggable: false`**                                                                                   | Rejected, even in a grid with `isDraggable: true`. An item with `isDraggable: true` starts in a grid with `isDraggable: false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | An item that renders as interactive is rejected, or the reverse.                                                      | Nothing, as today.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| **Concurrent render discarded**                                                                                     | Ref mirrors are written in layout effects through `useLatest`, not during render (`GridLayout.tsx:407,424,438` today).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | A handler reads a value from a render that never committed.                                                           | Nothing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### External drop

| State or input                                                                                      | What the operation does                                                                                                                        | How it can fail                                                                           | What the caller is told                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Drop enters the grid**                                                                            | `start{kind:"drop", item, at}` places the placeholder at the entry cell.                                                                       | The drop lifecycle leaks simulated drag callbacks or reports the wrong entry cell.        | dropConfig hover callback only; no drag callbacks at any phase (RX-11a, BREAK 2).                                                                                                  |
| **Drop committed**                                                                                  | The stop pipeline runs; the commit is the working layout without the placeholder (or with it renamed to `dropId` on the hook path).            | The placeholder id reaches `onLayoutChange` and loops a controlled parent (#2210).        | `onDrop(layout with placeholder, item, e)`, as today (`GridLayout.tsx:1037-1042`). One `onLayoutChange` without the placeholder. No drag callbacks; legacy retains its v1 mapping. |
| **Child added or removed, or the controlled layout updated, during a drop hover**                   | Queue rule 1 does not apply to a drop. A removal follows rule 2 and every other change rule 3, so the drop continues.                          | Every hover-time sync cancels the drop with `itemRemoved`.                                | `onDrop` once, no cancelled stop, one `onLayoutChange` without the placeholder.                                                                                                    |
| **External drop leaves the grid** (today: pinned, `external-leave.vertical`)                        | The drop controller dispatches `cancel("dropLeft")`. The engine removes the placeholder and reverts. All drop state is cleared (fixes RX-11b). | The placeholder is stuck. The refs leak. `onDrop` fires for a drop that never happened.   | No drag callbacks, onDrop or onLayoutChange. The engine cancel detail stays internal; legacy preserves its v1 drop sequence.                                                       |
| **External drop released outside the grid** (touch; today: pinned, `external-touch-leave.vertical`) | Today `touchend` off the grid removes the placeholder (`GridLayout.tsx:1121-1134`). The controller dispatches `cancel("dropLeft")`.            |                                                                                           | Same as "leaves the grid".                                                                                                                                                         |
| **Drop hover rejected** (`onDragOver` or `onTouchDragOver` returns `false`)                         | No placeholder, no `start{kind:"drop"}` (`GridLayout.tsx:950-952` today).                                                                      |                                                                                           | Nothing, as today.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| **Touch drop with an existing `onDragOver` that reads `e.dataTransfer`**                            | The touch path never calls `onDragOver`, whose contract is `DragEvent` (`core/types.ts:562-563`). It calls `onTouchDragOver` if passed.        | `e.dataTransfer` is undefined on a `TouchEvent`, the callback throws and the drop aborts. | Nothing. Touch overrides need `onTouchDragOver` (RX-11c).                                                                                                                          |

### Interaction middleware

| State or input                                                                                  | What the operation does                                                                                                                                                   | How it can fail                                                                    | What the caller is told                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `onProposal` returns `false` at `start`                                                         | No interaction begins. `active` stays `null`. The binding ignores the gesture until release.                                                                              | RD keeps dragging and later moves start a drag.                                    | Nothing. `onDragStart` does not fire, so no stop fires either.                                                                                                          |
| `onProposal` returns an edit at a drag or resize `start`                                        | Ignored; only `false` counts there, because no step runs at that start.                                                                                                   | The edit moves the item before any step.                                           | `RGL_W012` in development.                                                                                                                                              |
| **Veto after activation** (delay or distance rule)                                              | Activation dispatched `start`, and middleware vetoed it after RD's start callback had returned (`DraggableCore.js:128`). The binding uses the ignore-until-release state. | The gesture keeps feeding moves.                                                   | Nothing. No start, so no stop.                                                                                                                                          |
| `onProposal` or `onResolved` returns `false` at `move`                                          | The step is dropped. State stays at the last accepted step, `lastProposal` included (§3).                                                                                 | The placeholder moves anyway, or the release re-runs the rejected proposal.        | `onDrag` does not fire for that step.                                                                                                                                   |
| **Middleware returns `false` at `stop`**                                                        | Cancel semantics: the committed layout stays `startLayout`, `pending` is flushed.                                                                                         | A consumer that mirrored `onDrag` layouts keeps them.                              | Stop callback (cancelled) with `cancelReason: "vetoed"` and `detail.rejectedBy`. Argument 1 is `startLayout`, so a consumer that stores it resets. No `onLayoutChange`. |
| `onProposal` returns an edit                                                                    | Fields outside the kind (`w`/`h` on a drag, `x`/`y` on a resize) are ignored. Validation, then constraints, then validation again.                                        | A fractional or out-of-bounds value reaches a compactor (C-B4 throws on `h: 1.5`). | `RGL_W005` for ignored fields. The detail shows the final `proposed`.                                                                                                   |
| `onResolved` returns a `Layout`                                                                 | Validated (same id set, finite integers, in bounds), then used without a second compaction.                                                                               | It drops or adds ids, or holds NaN.                                                | Invalid: `RGL_W005`, in production too, and the step counts as a veto.                                                                                                  |
| **Middleware throws during an interaction**                                                     | The reducer catches it and returns an `error` event. The step counts as a veto. At `stop`, cancel semantics with `cancelReason: "error"`. Nothing is rethrown.            | The pointer listeners and engine state are left mid-interaction.                   | `onError(error, "middleware:<name>", detail)` if passed; otherwise `console.error` once per source and grid, in production too. At stop: stop callback (cancelled).     |
| **A strategy throws during sync** (`createInitialState`, `syncProps`, `setConfig`, `setLayout`) | Caught; `error` event with phase `"sync"`. The previous state is kept. `createInitialState` keeps the validated input layout without compaction.                          | The grid renders an unspecified layout, or the throw is lost.                      | `onError`, or `console.error` once per source and grid, in production too.                                                                                              |
| Middleware is slow                                                                              | Runs synchronously per proposal.                                                                                                                                          | Frames drop.                                                                       | Nothing. The op-count suite has a middleware-overhead case.                                                                                                             |

### `Compactor.resolveDrag` and the compact context

| State or input                                                 | What the operation does                                                                                                                  | How it can fail                                                  | What the caller is told                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `resolveDrag` returns `null`                                   | Move: the step is dropped. Stop: the last accepted working layout commits.                                                               | A normal stop is read as a rejection.                            | `detail.rejectedBy: "resolveDrag"` on the dropped step.                                                                 |
| `resolveDrag` mutates `ctx.layout`                             | Not checked at runtime. Test suites deep-freeze inputs, never dev builds (§11).                                                          | The committed layout changes behind the engine's back.           | Undefined behavior, documented. The `swapCompactor` spec shows how a third-party compactor freezes its inputs in tests. |
| `resolveDrag` returns a layout with missing or duplicate ids   | Id-set check.                                                                                                                            | Items vanish.                                                    | `RGL_W005`; the step counts as a veto.                                                                                  |
| `resolveDrag` or `compact` throws                              | Same as middleware: an `error` event, and the step counts as a veto.                                                                     |                                                                  | `onError`, or `console.error` once per source and grid.                                                                 |
| `resolveDrag` returns overlaps while `allowOverlap` is `false` | Not checked at runtime (an O(n^2) check per frame).                                                                                      | Overlapping items.                                               | Nothing. The golden invariant suite checks built-ins.                                                                   |
| 3.0 custom compactor without required `resolveDrag`            | It fails the 3.0 consumer compile fixture. There is no runtime type-based fallback; the guide supplies explicit classicResolveDrag axes. | A custom horizontal or wrap compactor silently drags vertically. | Type error; migrate via BREAK 1 before running the 3.0 package.                                                         |
| Resize with a swap compactor                                   | Resize never calls `resolveDrag`; `resizeElement` runs.                                                                                  | A resize turns into a swap.                                      | Nothing.                                                                                                                |
| A two-argument compactor gets `ctx`                            | Ignored by design.                                                                                                                       |                                                                  | Nothing.                                                                                                                |

### `LayoutItem.data`

| State or input                        | What the operation does                                                                         | How it can fail                               | What the caller is told                      |
| ------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- |
| `data` holds a large or cyclic object | Copied by reference, never cloned or walked. `sameInput` compares only its reference.           | A deep comparison walks it on every commit.   | Nothing. Documented.                         |
| Inline `data` objects every render    | `sameInput` differs, so each render syncs; `sameGeometry` matches, so there is no notification. | A notify loop.                                | Nothing.                                     |
| Consumer mutates `data` in place      | Same reference everywhere.                                                                      | The change does not reach state as new input. | Documented: replace the object to update it. |

### Render slots (`renderPlaceholder`, `renderItem`)

| State or input                                            | What the operation does                            | How it can fail                 | What the caller is told                                                                  |
| --------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `renderPlaceholder` returns `null`                        | The wrapper stays, empty.                          |                                 | Nothing.                                                                                 |
| `renderPlaceholder` or `renderItem` throws                | A normal React render error.                       | The grid unmounts mid-drag.     | The nearest error boundary. The grid-unmount row applies.                                |
| Inline `renderItem`, or `renderItem` reading parent state | Called on every `GridLayout` render; no cache.     | Stale content from a cache.     | Nothing. Items re-render with `GridLayout`; consumers memoize their content to avoid it. |
| Container width changes                                   | `position` changes, and the next render passes it. | `renderItem` sees a stale size. | Nothing.                                                                                 |
| `renderItem` content changes height                       | Not measured. RGL sizes from the layout.           | Overflow.                       | Documented. Auto-height items are out of scope.                                          |

### Activation rules

| State or input                                                     | What the operation does                                                                                                                                                                                               | How it can fail                                                                                | What the caller is told                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Touch delay rule, finger moves past `tolerance` before `delay`** | `decideActivation` returns `cancel`, and the binding's `touchmove` listener stops calling `preventDefault`. RD got `allowMobileScroll`, so it never called `preventDefault` on `touchstart` (`DraggableCore.js:105`). | The page cannot scroll, or the browser already gave the gesture away on the first `touchmove`. | Nothing. No `onDragStart`. Checked by hand on iOS Safari and Android Chrome in the rc (§4 rank 8). |
| Touch delay rule, finger holds still                               | The timer fires and activates. The `touchmove` listener calls `preventDefault` synchronously from then on.                                                                                                            | No move event, so it never activates; or `preventDefault` from the timer, which does nothing.  | Normal callbacks.                                                                                  |
| A delay rule, and the item unmounts during the wait                | The timer is cleared in the item's cleanup.                                                                                                                                                                           | The timer fires and dispatches after unmount.                                                  | Nothing.                                                                                           |
| `threshold` and `activation` both set                              | `activation` wins.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |                                                                                                | `RGL_W006` once.                                                                                   |

### Keyboard

| State or input                                                         | What the operation does                                                              | How it can fail                            | What the caller is told                                                |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Nudge a static item                                                    | The reducer rejects the `nudge`. Nothing changes.                                    |                                            | `announce("Item is locked")` when set. No callbacks.                   |
| Nudge into a static item, or with `preventCollision`                   | The same pipeline as a pointer step rejects it.                                      |                                            | `announce` with the reason. No callbacks and no `onLayoutChange`.      |
| A nudge rejected after its start (a veto at `move` or `stop`, a throw) | The nudge is atomic: the previous state stays and no interaction events are emitted. | `onDragStart` fires with no matching stop. | Nothing; `announce` gives the reason. A throw still reaches `onError`. |
| Arrow key inside an `<input>` in an item                               | Ignored: `target !== currentTarget`, or `defaultPrevented`.                          | Typing moves the widget.                   | Nothing.                                                               |
| Focus leaves mid keyboard move                                         | Each press commits at once, so nothing is pending.                                   |                                            | Nothing.                                                               |

### Edge scroll

| State or input                           | What the operation does                                                    | How it can fail                               | What the caller is told |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| No scrollable ancestor of the grid       | Falls back to `window` (RX-10c).                                           | Nothing scrolls.                              | Nothing.                |
| Pointer holds still at an edge           | The binding recomputes the proposal from the new scroll offset each frame. | The item stays at stale coordinates (RX-10d). | Normal `onDrag` calls.  |
| The scroll container is removed mid-drag | The loop stops on the next frame when the node is disconnected.            | The rAF loop runs forever (RX-10a).           | Nothing.                |

### Untrusted layout input (§14)

| State or input                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | What the operation does                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | How it can fail                                                                                                            | What the caller is told                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Large finite value on each intake path** (2.4.0): `layout` prop, `data-grid`, stored `ResponsiveGridLayout` `layouts`, `useGridLayout` initial layout and `setLayout`, `useResponsiveLayout` `layouts`, `setLayoutForBreakpoint` and `setLayouts`, the drop item and its `onDragOver` override | `validateLayoutInput` clamps `w` and `minW` to `[1, cols]`, `x` to `[0, cols - w]`, `h` and `minH` to `[1, rowLimit]` and a finite `y` to `[0, rowLimit]`, where `rowLimit` is `MAX_GRID_ROWS` (10,000) or a smaller finite `maxRows`, at least 1. Responsive paths use each breakpoint's `cols`. One test per path. | A path skips validation, and a saved layout with `h: 1e9` freezes the tab (C-W2).                                          | one development `console.warn` per clamping call (`RGL_W002` from RGL-47).                              |
| **Item size limits** (2.4.0): `minH: 1e9` with an otherwise valid item                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Clamped like `h`, so a later resize cannot restore an oversized height through `applySizeConstraints` (`core/constraints.ts:95`).                                                                                                                                                                                    | Validation passes, then the first resize produces `h: 1e9`.                                                                | The intake warning.                                                                                     |
| **Item maxima** (2.4.0): `maxW` or `maxH` above the grid, `Infinity`, `null` or missing                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Passed unchanged and silently. `Infinity` is the documented default, and a maximum above the grid causes no work, because `w` and `h` are bounded on their own. A maximum below its minimum is raised to it.                                                                                                         | Clamping rewrites `Infinity` into every `onLayoutChange` payload, and a later, wider grid stays capped.                    | Nothing.                                                                                                |
| **Non-finite item limits** (2.4.0): `minH: NaN`, `maxW: NaN`, a string                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `NaN`, `-Infinity` and non-numbers take the lower bound: 1 for `minW`/`minH`, the minimum (or 1) for `maxW`/`maxH`.                                                                                                                                                                                                  | The first resize turns `minH: NaN` into `h: NaN` (`core/constraints.ts:95`), and collision loops spin.                     | The intake warning.                                                                                     |
| Non-finite `x`, `y`, `w` or `h` (2.4.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `NaN`, `-Infinity` and non-numbers take the lower bound (`x`, `y` to 0; `w`, `h` to 1); `+Infinity` takes the upper bound. The documented `y: Infinity` (bottom) is kept, and `correctBounds` resolves it against the bottom of the items with a finite `y` (RGL-49).                                                | `collides` returns `true` for NaN and loops spin (C-L1).                                                                   | The intake warning.                                                                                     |
| `x` outside the current `cols` (2.4.0), negative or past the right edge                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Clamped to `[0, cols - w]` at intake, so `correctBounds`' pinned `x: 0, w: cols` rule (C-B10) is reached only by direct calls.                                                                                                                                                                                       | The item renders off the grid, or validation silently rewrites it.                                                         | The intake warning, and an `onLayoutChange` for the moved item. Changelog.                              |
| Duplicate `i` (2.4.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Kept as today (C-B14). #2328 does not check ids; RGL-47 adds the check.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Both items compact to the same cell.                                                                                       | `RGL_W007` (RGL-47).                                                                                    |
| **Overlapping statics, h = 10,000** (2.4.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `correctBounds` resolves each static with one sorted sweep over its x-overlapping colliders (RGL-49): O(n log n) per static, O(n^2 log n) in total. No item-count cap, because a cap drops user data.                                                                                                                | Cubic work in S (C-W1). With only the round-2 jump fix, 1,000 overlapping statics took 558 ms on the #2328 head (round 3). | Nothing. Budget scenarios at n = 80 and n = 1,000 statics: 0 `collides` calls in both (#2328 baseline). |
| **Wrap compactor with a tall static or a tall movable item** (2.4.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Interval occupancy; blocked spans are jumped, never enumerated (RGL-22).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | A billion iterations, or a billion `Set` entries, or a movable item placed over the static.                                | Nothing. Budget tests, and a non-overlap check, for `cols: 1` with a static `h: 1e9` and one 1x1 item.  |
| **Classic compactor with a large `y` below a tall item** (2.4.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | One pass finds the stop row (RGL-49).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | One `collides` scan per row stepped (C-W2).                                                                                | Nothing. Budget scenario `large-y`: 2 `collides` calls with `h` and `y` at 1e9 (#2328 baseline).        |
| **A static and a movable item at `y: Infinity`** (2.4.0)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `correctBounds` computes the bottom over items with a finite `y` only (C-W3).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Both items stay at `Infinity`, or the upward loop never ends.                                                              | Nothing. Budget test: both end on finite rows.                                                          |
| **Direct calls to exported compaction code** (2.4.0): `compactItemVertical`, `compactItemHorizontal`, `fastHorizontalCompactor.compact`                                                                                                                                                          | The axis helpers find the stop row in one pass (RGL-49), and the fast horizontal tide no longer allocates per row (RGL-22).                                                                                                                                                                                          | Work or memory proportional to a coordinate, outside intake (C-W4).                                                        | Nothing. Budget tests call the public exports.                                                          |
