## 11. Testing Strategy

Each layer below sits on the golden masters already decided. None of them replaces a golden.

### 1. Golden masters (decided, merged)

- **RGL core** (F1): every row of rgl-core §1.2, interaction scripts through `moveElement` + `compact`, and fast-vs-classic differentials, recorded as known divergences. The serializer keeps `undefined`, `±Infinity`, `NaN`, holes and order. It records `out` and `inputAfter` separately, plus reference identity (rgl-core §1.0).
- **RGL React** (RGL-03, #2321): `test/golden-react/`, jsdom traces for each scenario and variant, through real react-draggable and react-resizable handlers, with no mocks of either (rgl-tests §6b). Each step records callbacks, styles, classes, the placeholder and Profiler commit counts (`test/golden-react/README.md` on master).
- **Enforcement**: an ordinary run fails on any changed or missing fixture; only `GOLDEN_UPDATE=1` writes. The PR description explains each diff, and the template asks for it (RGL-45, §15).
- **RD and RR** (F2, F3): jsdom traces, one `act()` per event, with the fake layout engine for RR's w/n correction (resizable §7), plus browser cross-checks.

### 2. Invariants, scoped by operation and input domain

A careless `golden-update` cannot hide these (rgl-tests §6a). Each invariant names the operations and inputs it covers. A case outside that domain is a plain golden fixture, so behavior pinned in §7 is not rejected by an invariant. On 2.4 and the private legacy path, correctBounds moves overlapping statics (core/layout.ts:230-232, C-B9). Modern 3.0 correctBounds never moves statics (BREAK 13), including overlapping ones; the no-overlap invariant still excludes layouts with overlapping statics.

| Invariant                                                          | Operations                                                 | Input domain                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The same set of ids in and out                                     | every core function, every reducer step                    | all, except duplicate-id fixtures (C-B14)                                                                                                      |
| Compaction preserves `w`, `h` and `static`                         | `compact` of every compactor                               | all                                                                                                                                            |
| No overlaps                                                        | `compact` with `allowOverlap: false`                       | inputs whose statics do not overlap each other; finite integers                                                                                |
| Statics never move                                                 | compact, resolveDrag; modern correctBounds from 3.0        | compact/resolveDrag: non-overlapping statics; modern correctBounds: all statics, even overlapping                                              |
| `0 <= x` and `x + w <= cols`                                       | `compact`, `resolveDrag`, `resizeElement`                  | items with `w <= cols`                                                                                                                         |
| Idempotence: `compact(compact(L))` equals `compact(L)`             | classic compactors                                         | finite integers                                                                                                                                |
| No input is mutated (deep-frozen with `test/util/deepFreeze.js`)   | every core function from RGL-11 and RGL-14 on; the reducer | all                                                                                                                                            |
| At most one `onLayoutChange` per commit                            | the reducer and every React trace                          | all                                                                                                                                            |
| Every `onDragStart`/`onResizeStart` gets exactly one stop callback | the reducer and every React trace                          | all modern item drag and resize callbacks, except grid unmount; external drops emit no drag callbacks; legacy v1 sequence is tested separately |

### 3. Engine reducer tables

Pure Jest, no React and no DOM. Each case is an action list in, with `state.layout` and `events` out, stored as golden files. The React scenarios from RGL-03 are replayed at grid-unit level through `reduceGrid`, and the results must equal the component traces, except the mid-interaction traces RGL-23 lists as expected diffs (RGL-23 acceptance).

### 4. Differential tests

| Pair                                                                                               | What must match                                                          | Where                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `useGridLayout` vs `GridLayout`                                                                    | Final layouts and callback sequences for every scenario                  | `test/golden-react/scenarios/differential.golden-test.tsx` (#2321). Diverges today (RX-9); equal after RGL-25.                                                                                            |
| Engine replay vs component trace                                                                   | Layouts and events at grid-unit level                                    | RGL-23                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `fastVerticalCompactor` vs `verticalCompactor`, `fastHorizontalCompactor` vs `horizontalCompactor` | Outputs over the corpus                                                  | `known-divergences.json`. RGL-22 shrinks it. No PR may grow it without review.                                                                                                                            |
| `sameGeometry` vs `fast-equals` `deepEqual`                                                        | Equal on every layout without `data`, except distinct constraint objects | RGL-23 (RX-19); already-normalized corpus plus JSON round trips and N-7; RX-17 cloning differences are separately approved in RGL-50                                                                      |
| Legacy wrapper vs the post-2.4 v1 baseline                                                         | Traces                                                                   | One legacy run per scenario; modern 3.0 differences map to §12 rather than requiring cross-version equality                                                                                               |
| jsdom trace vs Playwright trace                                                                    | Grid-unit layouts, pixel fields excluded                                 | RGL-06. Each mismatch is recorded in `known-browser-divergences.json` with an issue, like `known-divergences.json`. A mismatch means the jsdom geometry stubs are wrong, or the behavior is browser-only. |
| RD at 4.5.0 vs RD at HEAD                                                                          | Trace diff, recorded                                                     | Documents D-ctrl and the 4.6.0 changes (draggable §7)                                                                                                                                                     |

### 5. Playwright scenario suite (RGL-06)

- `test/e2e/harness-app.jsx` takes `?scenario=<id>&variant=<v>` and renders the shared scenario definitions from `test/golden-react/`, wrapped in StrictMode when the variant says so. It exposes `window.__rgl = { ready, trace, frames, layout, cellToClient }`.
- Pointer moves use `page.mouse` with `{ steps }`, aimed at grid-unit targets computed in the page. No hard-coded pixels and no `waitForTimeout`. Waits use `expect.poll` on frame counts.
- Artifacts per test: `trace.json` with real `getBoundingClientRect` per item, screenshots at start, mid-gesture and end, and the Playwright trace. CI uploads `playwright-report/` and `test-results/` with `if: always()`.
- Browser-only scenarios: breakpoint crossing through `setViewportSize`; edge scroll in `#scroll-container`; HTML5 drop with a `DataTransfer`; a CSS-scaled container; touch through a `hasTouch` context with in-page `TouchEvent`s (the two `fixme` tests at `touch-drop.spec.ts:33,99` use CDP, which does not fire DOM touch events in headless Chromium).
- **Per-frame pixel checks.** Separately from the grid-unit comparison, every drag and resize scenario records the item's `getBoundingClientRect` at each mid-gesture frame and asserts it tracks the pointer, for every handle, with scale and padding variants. This is the RX-1 class, which jsdom cannot see. The boxes, rounded to 0.01 px, are committed as JSON under `test/e2e/__frames__/`, recorded on the Linux CI image, so "byte-identical" has a baseline. A frame is one sample after each awaited `page.mouse.move` settles, never an rAF tick, and only the `workflow_dispatch` recorder job on the Linux image writes the JSON. Chromium only in 2.4.0. RGL-24's first commit adds WebKit and Firefox projects and records their JSON on the 2.4.x code, so the engine commits are checked against it (§15). `test/e2e/live-interaction.spec.ts` is a required CI check from RGL-07 on.
- **Rotated strategy.** One scenario drives the README's rotated `PositionStrategy` (`README.md:1028-1029`) and pins its per-frame boxes (RGL-27).
- A `dist` project resolves `react-grid-layout` through the `package.json` `exports` map after `yarn build` and runs the smoke scenarios against ESM and CJS.

### 6. CI matrix (RGL-07)

| Axis              | Values                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| React / react-dom | 18.3.x, 19.x                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| react-draggable   | 4.5.0 (floor after RGL-08), latest 4.x                                                                                                                                                          |
| react-resizable   | 4.0.2 (floor after RGL-08) only; latest 4.x joins with the RR 4.1.0 gate, when the axis first has two values (§8)                                                                               |
| Node              | one version for every job, read from `.nvmrc` (Node 24, RGL-45)                                                                                                                                 |
| Suites per cell   | contract, interaction golden, SSR. The RD-latest and RR-latest cells also run Playwright smoke and `live-interaction.spec.ts`. The full Jest suite and full Playwright run on the default cell. |

React 19 depends on RGL-04: `test/spec/lifecycle-test.js:6` imports `react-dom/test-utils` for 28 `Simulate` calls, and React 19 removed `Simulate` (rgl-tests §3.2).

The matrix above applies to both release lines with their own package builds: 4 cells before the RR latest axis, then 8. 3.0 has a hard React/react-dom floor of `>= 18`, native `useSyncExternalStore` and `useId`, with no 16.8/17 smoke job or shim dependency. RGL-24 sets this floor and verifies SSR-stable keyboard ids.

2.4.x keeps the `>= 16.8.0` peer range. RGL-07 adds a best-effort smoke test on React 16.8 and 17, using ReactDOM.render, dispatched DOM events, renderToString and ReactDOM.hydrate without Testing Library. That test runs only on release/2.4 after the lines split; it is never a 3.0 compatibility claim.

### 6b. Type compatibility (RGL-46)

- **API report.** Generate it from the built declarations and fail on every unreviewed diff. 2.4 rejects breaking changes. 3.0 accepts only the signature/export changes mapped to §12; removed exports have negative consumer fixtures. CJS/ESM and K1-K7 remain covered.
- **2.x ledger.** Keep `test/types/consumer-2.3/*.ts` unchanged after RGL-46: exhaustive CompactType/compactor.type switches, mutable defaults, CSSProperties strategies, custom Compactor and callbacks. Compile it against each 2.4 build, with strict TS 5.0 and current TS. In the 3.0 lane, run it against the maintained 2.x package and record its expected 3.0 failures against the §12 ledger; do not weaken it to make 3.0 green.
- **3.0 fixtures.** Add `test/types/consumer-3.0/*.ts` next to that ledger in RGL-46. Run against built 3.0: name plus required resolveDrag, readonly defaults/items, PositionStrategy.itemClassName, dropConfig.item, verb hook actions/ids, generic callbacks, root aliases and CompactType sorting. Test removed exports as expected compile errors. RGL-35 and RGL-50 update the 3.0 report and fixtures as their contracts land; RGL-51 compiles transformed consumers too.

### 7. SSR test (RGL-07)

`test/spec/ssr-test.tsx` with `/** @jest-environment node */`:

- `renderToString` of `GridLayout`, `ResponsiveGridLayout`, legacy RGL and `WidthProvider(measureBeforeMount)` must not throw and must not touch `window` or `navigator`.
- A jsdom twin hydrates the markup with `hydrateRoot` and asserts no mismatch warnings.
- From RGL-24, the store's `getServerSnapshot` returns the initial engine state. RGL-07 has no store yet, so the assertion lands with it.

### 8. Dist and exports test (RGL-07)

`test/spec/exports-test.ts` runs after `yarn build`:

- Each `exports` entry (`.`, `./core`, `./react`, `./legacy`, `./extras`, `./css/styles.css`) resolves under both `import` and `require`.
- `react-resizable/css/styles.css`, which the README imports (`README.md:202,209`), resolves with RR at the floor and at latest. RR 4.0.2 ships `css/` in its `files` list (RR `package.json:7-9`).
- Snapshot of each entry's exported names. 2.4 rejects removed names. 3.0 permits only the exact §12 removals and tests that K1-K7 remain available.
- `./core` has no `react` import in the built output (Clarity rule 10).
- `node --input-type=module -e "import 'react-grid-layout'"`, and the same for each sub-entry, run after the build with no bundler. An invalid built-package import fails here instead of in a consumer's server. 3.0 has no use-sync-external-store shim path to resolve.

### 9. Flakiness rules

These address rgl-tests §4:

- No wall-clock assertions in Jest (#2320). Op counts gate instead (RGL-02).
- Every random generator is seeded, and the seed is printed on failure.
- No real timers. Fake timers only.
- Global prototype patches go through `installGridGeometry` with restore in `afterEach`.
- The golden harness depends on the global timsort patch (`test/util/setupTests.js:5-9`). Every job runs on the pinned Node, and fixtures where a comparator can return NaN (`y: Infinity`) are flagged, since only there can tie order differ (RGL-45).
- The e2e port is not 4002, or the server is not reused outside CI.

## 12. Breaking changes in 3.0

Sam's ruling is "I want more breaking changes if needed". These 14 BREAK groups buy clarity, testability or third-party pluggability. Each has a migration below. `/legacy` stays in-package, frozen at v1 behavior, and absorbs only the changes marked here. Its React peer floor still rises with the package.

New citations in revision 5 were read in this checkout at `2182f06a`; they override the older audit citation base for these rows. Proposed APIs and PR files are design, not claims that the implementation already exists.

| BREAK | Old -> new                                                                                                                                                     | Why (current code evidence)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Migration                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Codemod                                                                                     | Legacy absorbs                                                                                                            |
| ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1     | `Compactor.type: CompactType` -> `name: string`; required `resolveDrag`                                                                                        | The closed identity (`core/types.ts:262-295`) selects drag behavior (`GridLayout.tsx:372`; `core/interaction/resolveDrag.ts:39-50,95-100`). Third parties need their own identity and drag semantics                                                                                                                                                                                | Rename the field; add `ctx => classicResolveDrag(ctx, axis)` with explicit wrap ordering where needed. Keep `CompactType` for sorting and legacy props                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Yes for recognized built-in axes; unknown implementations flagged                           | Yes. Private legacy mapping builds complete compactors                                                                    |
| 2     | Simulated drag callbacks for external drops -> drop lifecycle only                                                                                             | `GridItem.tsx:799-833` fabricates drag starts/moves; the committed drop has no matching drag stop (RX-11a). Removing the simulated callbacks makes pairing total                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Move hover UI to `dropConfig.onDragOver` or `onTouchDragOver`; keep commit handling in `onDrop`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | No, semantic                                                                                | Yes. Private mapping preserves the v1 callback sequence, including no stop on committed drop                              |
| 3     | Public `GridItem`, `GridItemProps`, `GridItemCallback` -> internal                                                                                             | Exported from root (`src/index.ts:16-25`) and `/react` (`react/index.ts:12-21`); its positioning flags (`GridItem.tsx:120-122,359-383`) are live for direct callers without a strategy and constrain binding changes                                                                                                                                                                | Use `GridLayout` with `renderItem`. Standalone `GridItem` has no direct replacement; compose a one-item grid or own positioning. For percentage left/width, use the explicit strategy migration below; plain `absoluteStrategy` keeps pixel units                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | No                                                                                          | N/A. Legacy exports no GridItem (`legacy/index.ts:12-41`)                                                                 |
| 4     | Old core helpers and string compactor APIs -> pure resolver/compactor objects                                                                                  | `moveElement` and `moveElementAwayFromCollision` take positional args including unused `_cols` (`core/layout.ts:265-317`); mutation helpers return an item or void (`core/compactors.ts:35-92`); the throwing structural validator needs a distinct name from intake normalization (`core/layout.ts:330-361`); string/object responsive dispatch (`core/responsive.ts:116,148-155`) | Remove public `moveElement`, `moveElementAwayFromCollision`, `resolveCompactionCollision`, `compactItemVertical`, `compactItemHorizontal`, `validateLayout`, `getCompactor`, and the `CompactType` argument of `findOrGenerateResponsiveLayout`. Use `classicResolveDrag`/`resolveDrag`, pure `compact` and direct compactor imports. Rejecting callers use `assertLayoutStructure` (§12 below); `validateLayoutInput` clamps and returns a layout. Rewrite mutation-dependent custom algorithms | Yes for recognized string mappings; no for validator calls or mutation-dependent algorithms | Yes for private `getCompactor` and string mapping; other utility removals do not affect the v1 component surface          |
| 5     | Mutable shared defaults and item fields -> readonly defaults, frozen shared values, readonly `LayoutItem` fields                                               | `defaultConstraints` is a shared mutable array (`core/constraints.ts:381`; `GridLayout.tsx:305`); configs (`core/types.ts:468-592`) and breakpoint maps (`useResponsiveLayout.ts:34-49`) are mutable; item fields are writable (`core/types.ts:37-107`)                                                                                                                             | Spread defaults into owned arrays/objects and replace items instead of mutating them. The counts are 14 BREAK groups, including 4's helper removals and 9's drop-config consolidation. The six overlap exports become one retained export plus five removed exports. BREAK 13 changes C-B9 and C-B10 only; C-B8 stays deferred.

### Validator and overlap migrations

`assertLayoutStructure(layout, contextName = "Layout")` is the throwing validator, added to `/core` in 2.4 by RGL-47. It keeps the checks and error messages in `core/layout.ts:330-361`: the input must be an array; defined items need numeric, non-NaN `x`, `y`, `w` and `h`; a present `i` must be a string. It returns `void` and never changes input. It retains the old acceptance of numeric `Infinity` and out-of-bounds numbers. `validateLayout` remains an alias through 2.4.x and is removed in 3.0. Rejecting callers migrate manually to the assertion. `validateLayoutInput` is a separate bounds normalizer (§14); using its return value silently repairs input and does not preserve the throwing contract. RGL-51 reports validator calls for manual review.

The five removed overlap variants need these separate migrations. The three clone-only implementations keep positions; both fast variants compute new positions. The codemod reports all five for manual review, including direct `.compact` calls and code that reads the old identity.

| Removed export                   | Current position behavior                                                                                                                                        | 3.0 migration                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Codemod           |
| -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------- |
| `verticalOverlapCompactor`       | Clones without moving (`core/compactors.ts:150-158`)                                                                                                             | `noOverlapCompactor` keeps positions                                                                                                                                                                                     | No, manual review |
| `horizontalOverlapCompactor`     | Clones without moving (`core/compactors.ts:163-170`)                                                                                                             | `noOverlapCompactor` keeps positions                                                                                                                                                                                     | No, manual review |
| `wrapOverlapCompactor`           | Clones without moving (`extras/wrapCompactor.ts:148-156`)                                                                                                        | `noOverlapCompactor` from `/core` keeps positions                                                                                                                                                                        | No, manual review |
| `fastVerticalOverlapCompactor`   | Sorts and closes positive gaps upward; an isolated item at `y: 5` moves to `y: 0` (`extras/fastVerticalCompactor.ts:66-77,109-112,179-188`)                      | Manual. Preserve the arrangement in consumer code before passing layout to `noOverlapCompactor`, or explicitly accept skip-compaction positions. A spread with `allowOverlap: true` does not preserve the fast algorithm | No, semantic      |
| `fastHorizontalOverlapCompactor` | Sorts, places at the row tide and wraps; it can push items right even with overlap allowed (`extras/fastHorizontalCompactor.ts:105-110,155-175,229-243,272-281`) | Manual. Preserve the arrangement in consumer code before passing layout to `noOverlapCompactor`, or explicitly accept skip-compaction positions. Review C-B4d against the post-RGL-22 baseline                           | No, semantic      |

Six overlap exports become one retained export and five removals: three position-preserving migrations and two semantic migrations. RGL-22 documents and pins the 2.4 meanings before RGL-50 consolidates them. No replacement with `noOverlapCompactor` or `{...c, allowOverlap: true}` preserves either fast variant's compaction behavior.

### Deprecations in 2.4

The only 2.x minor in this plan is 2.4. A deprecation is actionable there only if its replacement ships there too. Core utilities get JSDoc only because they can run in hot loops; the code in the table identifies the notice in docs and lint output. Props/config get a once-per-grid development warning. No behavior is removed from 2.4.x.

| 2.4 deprecation                                                                                                     | Replacement available in 2.4                                                                                            | Warning code | Delivery / removal                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `validateLayout` (`core/layout.ts:330`)                                                                             | `assertLayoutStructure(layout, contextName?)`, added in RGL-47 with the same throwing checks                            | `RGL_W013`   | JSDoc only; old name removed in 3.0. Manual migration preserves rejection; never replace it with clamping                                                          |
| Public `getCompactor` and responsive string argument (`core/compactors.ts:200`; `core/responsive.ts:116`)           | Direct compactor objects                                                                                                | `RGL_W008`   | JSDoc for general calls; runtime warning remains for `getCompactor("wrap")`; removed in 3.0                                                                        |
| `onDropDragOver` (`GridLayout.tsx:181`)                                                                             | `dropConfig.onDragOver`, native event (`core/types.ts:562-567`)                                                         | `RGL_W014`   | One dev warning on the modern entry, no legacy warning; removed in 3.0                                                                                             |
| Modern-entry `data-grid` (`GridLayout.tsx:247-266`)                                                                 | Explicit `layout`/`layouts`                                                                                             | `RGL_W015`   | One dev warning, legacy excluded; removed in 3.0                                                                                                                   |
| Five named overlap variants (core/extras citations above)                                                           | Per-export table above: three clone-only variants map to `noOverlapCompactor`; both fast variants need manual migration | `RGL_W016`   | JSDoc only; removed in 3.0. 2.4 keeps every variant's behavior; no blanket codemod rewrite                                                                         |
| `droppingItem` (`GridLayout.tsx:134`)                                                                               | `dropConfig.defaultItem`, extended additively with optional `i` in 2.4, then renamed `item` in 3.0                      | `RGL_W017`   | One dev warning on modern entry; removed in 3.0                                                                                                                    |
| Direct GridItem `usePercentages`/`useCSSTransforms` flags, live without a strategy (`GridItem.tsx:120-122,359-383`) | `transformStrategy` or `absoluteStrategy`; percentage left/width needs the custom `calcStyle` described below           | `RGL_W009`   | Dev warning for explicit direct-use flags; internal defaults and legacy excluded. Keep their behavior through 2.4.x; removed with GridItem export in 3.0 (BREAK 3) |

For direct `GridItem` use in 2.4, `positionStrategy.calcStyle` takes precedence over both flags (`GridItem.tsx:359-383`). Use `transformStrategy` for transforms and `absoluteStrategy` for pixel top/left. To preserve `useCSSTransforms={false}` with `usePercentages`, create a strategy from `absoluteStrategy` whose `calcStyle(pos)` returns its pixel style with `left: 100 * pos.left / containerWidth + "%"` and `width: 100 * pos.width / containerWidth + "%"`. Keep `top` and `height` in pixels. The closure uses the current `containerWidth`, on the server and on the client. In 3.0, pass the strategy to a one-item `GridLayout` with `renderItem`, or own the positioned element (BREAK 3); migrate the strategy identity under BREAK 10. RGL-53 includes a worked percentage example. The warning must describe this active behavior.

Seven actionable deprecation groups: 3 JSDoc-only groups + 4 prop/flag warning groups = 7. `getCompactor("wrap")` also retains its existing planned runtime warning. RGL-47 owns the notices; RGL-17 owns that factory warning.

`moveElement`, `moveElementAwayFromCollision`, the mutating `compactItem*`/`resolveCompactionCollision` helpers and `GridItem` have no 2.4 replacement. RGL-47 adds a 2.4 JSDoc `@deprecated` notice pointing forward to the 3.0 guide, but it is a migration announcement, not a runtime warning or a claim that `classicResolveDrag`/`renderItem` is available in 2.4. The drop-callback change is announced in the guide without a 2.4 warning. No threshold deprecation is added: `dragConfig.threshold` remains a supported alias in 3.0 (K7).

| KEEP | Contract and reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| K1   | Positional callbacks stay. This was the closest call: argument 1 varies by phase (`GridLayout.tsx:583,616`), but the seventh `InteractionDetail` argument covers #345's pre/post-compaction data. A single event object would rewrite every modern consumer without adding capability. Document cancelled-stop payloads.                                                                                                                          |

Arbitrary spread keys would pull user objects into geometry equality and recreate RX-17-class notifications.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Renaming consumer selectors adds churn.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

A separate package doubles release work and cannot evade the package React floor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

The pointer experiment's parity, minified bytes and reads are still to measure (§8); an internal swap can ship in 3.x or a later major once justified.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
